9	// Shared farmer-side order shape. Superset of the columns the dashboard and the
10	// Orders page each fetch, so the same card renders on both.
11	export type FarmerOrder = {
12	  id: string
13	  farmer_id: string
14	  order_code?: string | null
15	  produce_listing_id: string | null
16	  produce_name: string | null
17	  quantity: number | null

19	  total_price: number | null
20	  buyer_name: string | null
21	  buyer_phone: string | null
22	  pickup_location: string | null
23	  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined' | 'cancelled'
24	  payment_method?: string | null
25	  payment_status: string | null
26	  utr_number?: string | null
27	  decline_reason: string | null
28	  refund_status?: string | null
29	  refund_amount?: number | null
30	  refunded_at?: string | null
31	  created_at: string
32	  delivery_type?: 'self_pickup' | 'home_delivery' | 'courier' | null
33	  delivery_status?: DeliveryStatus | null
34	  delivery_boy_id?: string | null
35	  fulfillment_date?: string | null
36	  collected_at?: string | null
37	  shipped_at?: string | null
38	  received_at?: string | null
39	  // When the farmer acknowledged a buyer-cancelled order (moves it to history).
40	  acknowledged_at?: string | null
41	}
42	
43	// An approved order is "resolved" (and so leaves the farmer's active list) once:
44	//   self_pickup   → the buyer collected it (collected_at set)
45	//   courier       → the buyer confirmed receipt (received_at set); note a
46	//                   shipped-but-unreceived courier order stays active
47	//   home_delivery → rider flow: the rider delivered it (delivery_status
48	//                   'delivered'); farmer-ships flow: the buyer confirmed
49	//                   receipt (received_at set)
50	export function isResolved(o: FarmerOrder): boolean {
51	  // A buyer-cancelled order is NOT resolved until the farmer acknowledges it.
52	  // Until then it stays in the active list so the farmer sees the cancellation
53	  // instead of it silently dropping into history. Once acknowledged, it's done.
54	  if (o.status === 'cancelled') return !!o.acknowledged_at
55	  if (o.status !== 'approved') return false
56	  if (o.delivery_type === 'home_delivery') return o.delivery_status === 'delivered' || !!o.received_at
57	  if (o.delivery_type === 'courier') return !!o.received_at
58	  return !!o.collected_at
59	}
60

404	/* ─── Home-delivery rider tag ──────────────────────────────── */
405	function DeliveryTagForFarmer({ order }: { order: FarmerOrder }) {
406	  const [rider, setRider] = useState<{ name: string | null; phone: string } | null>(null)
407	  const riderId = order.delivery_boy_id ?? null
408	
409	  useEffect(() => {
410	    if (!riderId) { setRider(null); return }
411	    let cancelled = false
412	    supabase
413	      .from('delivery_boys')
414	      .select('name, phone')
415	      .eq('id', riderId)
416	      .maybeSingle()
417	      .then(({ data }) => {
418	        if (cancelled || !data) return
419	        setRider({ name: data.name ?? null, phone: data.phone as string })
420	      })
421	    return () => { cancelled = true }
422	  }, [riderId])

425	    switch (order.delivery_status) {
426	      case 'assigned': return 'Rider assigned'
427	      case 'picked_up': return 'Picked up'
428	      case 'out_for_delivery': return 'Out for delivery'
429	      case 'delivered': return 'Delivered'
430	      default: return 'Waiting for rider'
431	    }

455	        </p>

457	    </div>

11	type StatusFilter = 'all' | 'pending' | 'approved' | 'completed' | 'declined' | 'cancelled'
12	type TimeFilter = 'today' | 'week' | 'month' | 'all'
13	
14	// An order the farmer can still act on (approve/decline, mark picked-up/shipped,
15	// or acknowledge a buyer cancellation). Everything else is terminal history.
16	function isActionable(o: Order): boolean {
17	  if (o.status === 'pending') return true
18	  if (o.status === 'approved') return !isResolved(o)
19	  if (o.status === 'cancelled') return !o.acknowledged_at
20	  return false // declined is always terminal
21	}
22	
23	// Which status bucket an order belongs to (drives the status filter chips).
24	function bucketOf(o: Order): Exclude<StatusFilter, 'all'> {
25	  if (o.status === 'pending') return 'pending'
26	  if (o.status === 'declined') return 'declined'
27	  if (o.status === 'cancelled') return 'cancelled'
28	  // approved: resolved (collected / received / delivered) → completed, else awaiting
29	  return isResolved(o) ? 'completed' : 'approved'
30	}
31	
32	function OrdersPageInner() {

51	  useEffect(() => {
52	    const s = searchParams.get('status')
53	    if (s && ['pending', 'approved', 'completed', 'declined', 'cancelled'].includes(s)) {
54	      setStatusFilter(s as StatusFilter)

62	  // Silent refresh keeps the spinner only for the very first load.
63	  const load = useCallback(async (silent = false) => {

66	    setFarmerId(farmerId)

72	    const { data } = await supabase

74	      .select('id, farmer_id, order_code, produce_listing_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, buyer_name, buyer_phone, pickup_location, status, payment_method, payment_status, utr_number, decline_reason, refund_status, refund_amount, refunded_at, delivery_type, delivery_status, delivery_boy_id, collected_at, shipped_at, received_at, fulfillment_date, created_at, acknowledged_at')

84	  // Keep this page live: a buyer can cancel after it loaded. Without this the

93	      .on(

95	        { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'orders', filter: `farmer_id=eq.${farmerId}` },
96	        () => { void load(true) },
97	      )
98	      .subscribe()
99	    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel) }
100	  }, [farmerId, load])

102	  useEffect(() => {

133	      .eq('id', orderId)

427	        <DeclineSuccessSheet result={declineResult} onClose={() => setDeclineResult(null)} />
428	      )}
429	    </main>
430	  )
431	}

433	export default function OrdersPage() {
434	  return (
435	    <Suspense fallback={null}>
436	      <OrdersPageInner />
437	    </Suspense>
438	  )
439	}

441	/* ─── Read-only history card (terminal orders) ─────────────────── */
442	function HistoryCard({ order, onSetDate }: { order: Order; onSetDate: (date: string) => void }) {
443	  const { tx, L } = useLang()
444	  const isApproved = order.status === 'approved'
445	  const isCancelled = order.status === 'cancelled'
446	  const isDelivery = order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
447	  const isCourier = order.delivery_type === 'courier'
448	
449	  const timeStr = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
450	    day: 'numeric',
451	    month: 'short',
452	    hour: '2-digit',
453	    minute: '2-digit',
454	  })
455	
456	  const stamp = (iso: string | null | undefined) =>
457	    iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : ''
458	
459	  // Completion line for an approved order: the final milestone + its date.
460	  const isShippedFlow = isCourier || isDelivery
461	  const completion = !isApproved ? null
462	    : isShippedFlow
463	      ? order.received_at
464	        ? { text: `${L('✓ Received', '✓ అందుకున్నారు')} · ${stamp(order.received_at)}`, cls: 'text-green-700' }
465	        : order.shipped_at
466	          ? { text: `${L('🚚 Shipped', '🚚 షిప్ చేయబడింది')} · ${stamp(order.shipped_at)}`, cls: 'text-amber-700' }
467	          : null
468	      : order.collected_at
469	        ? { text: `${L('✓ Picked up', '✓ తీసుకువెళ్ళారు')} · ${stamp(order.collected_at)}`, cls: 'text-green-700' }
470	        : null
471	
472	  return (
473	    <div className={`rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden ${isApproved ? 'border-green-200 bg-white' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50'}`}>
474	      <div className="p-3 space-y-1.5">
475	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
476	          <div className="min-w-0">
477	            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
478	              <p className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight">
479	                {order.buyer_name || '—'}
480	              </p>
481	              <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
482	                isApproved ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
483	                  : isCancelled ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-700'
484	                  : 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
485	              }`}>
486	                {isApproved ? tx.statusApproved
487	                  : isCancelled ? L('Cancelled by buyer', 'కొనుగోలుదారు రద్దు చేశారు')
488	                  : tx.statusDeclined}
489	              </span>
490	            </div>
491	            {order.buyer_phone && (
492	              <a href={`tel:+91${order.buyer_phone}`} className="text-xs font-semibold text-green-700">
493	                📞 +91 {order.buyer_phone}
494	              </a>
495	            )}
496	          </div>
497	          <div className="flex flex-col items-end flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
498	            <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 whitespace-nowrap">
499	              {timeStr}
500	            </span>
501	            {order.order_code && (
502	              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold text-gray-400 whitespace-nowrap">
503	                {order.order_code}
504	              </span>
505	            )}
506	          </div>
507	        </div>
508	
509	        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
510	          <span className="font-semibold text-gray-800">{order.produce_name || '—'}</span>
511	          <span className="text-gray-300">·</span>
512	          <span className="text-gray-600">{order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}</span>
513	          {order.total_price != null && order.total_price > 0 && (
514	            <>
515	              <span className="text-gray-300">·</span>
516	              <span className="font-bold text-green-700">₹{order.total_price}</span>
517	            </>
518	          )}
519	        </div>
520	
521	        {order.pickup_location && (
522	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">📍 {order.pickup_location}</p>
523	        )}
524	
525	        {/* Completion status + date (picked up / shipped / received). */}
526	        {completion && (
527	          <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${completion.cls}`}>{completion.text}</p>
528	        )}
529	
530	        {/* Approved orders: farmer sets/edits the pickup-or-delivery date. */}
531	        {isApproved && (
532	          <div className="pt-1">
533	            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-600 block mb-1">
534	              📅 {isDelivery ? tx.deliveryDateLabel : tx.pickupDateLabel}
535	            </label>
536	            <input
537	              type="date"
538	              value={order.fulfillment_date ?? ''}
539	              onChange={(e) => onSetDate(e.target.value)}
540	              className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
541	            />
542	          </div>
543	        )}
544	
545	        {/* Declined orders: show the reason and the refund status to the farmer */}
546	        {!isApproved && order.decline_reason && (
547	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 leading-snug">
548	            <span className="font-semibold">{L('Reason', 'కారణం:')}</span> {order.decline_reason}
549	          </p>
550	        )}
551	        {!isApproved && (order.refund_status || (order.refund_amount ?? 0) > 0) && (
552	          <div className="mt-1 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5">
553	            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-green-800 flex items-center gap-1">
554	              {L('💸 Refund initiated to customer', 'రీఫండ్ ప్రారంభమైంది')}
555	            </p>
556	            <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 leading-snug mt-0.5">
557	              ₹{order.refund_amount ?? order.total_price ?? 0} · reflects in 3–5 business days
558	              {order.refunded_at && (
559	                <> · {new Date(order.refunded_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}</>
560	              )}
561	            </p>
562	          </div>
563	        )}
564	      </div>
565	    </div>
566	  )
567	}

73	export default function OrderDetailsPage() {
74	  const params = useParams<{ id: string }>()
75	  const id = typeof params?.id === 'string' ? params.id : ''
76	  const { state, openAuth } = useConsumerAuth()
77	  const { tx, L, lang } = useLang()
78	  const [order, setOrder] = useState<Order | null>(null)
79	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

82	  const [proofLoading, setProofLoading] = useState(false)
83	  const [showReceipt, setShowReceipt] = useState(false)
84	  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(false)
85	  const [showCancel, setShowCancel] = useState(false)
86	  const [showComplaint, setShowComplaint] = useState(false)
87	  const [confirmingReceipt, setConfirmingReceipt] = useState(false)
88	
89	  // Buyer may cancel only while still pending and within 30 min of placing.
90	  const canCancel = !!order
91	    && order.status === 'pending'
92	    && !!order.created_at
93	    && Date.now() - new Date(order.created_at).getTime() < 30 * 60 * 1000
94	
95	  // A home delivery is rider-driven once a rider is assigned (or its delivery
96	  // status moved past 'unassigned'). Those keep the rider tracking panel;
97	  // home deliveries with no rider are farmer-shipped (Shipped → Received).
98	  const riderAssigned = !!order
99	    && order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
100	    && (!!order.rider || (order.delivery_status != null && order.delivery_status !== 'unassigned'))
101	
102	  // A shipped order (courier, or a farmer-shipped home delivery) awaits the
103	  // buyer's "Received" confirmation. Rider deliveries never set shipped_at, so
104	  // they're naturally excluded; self-pickup is gated out explicitly.
105	  const canConfirmReceipt = !!order
106	    && order.delivery_type !== 'self_pickup'
107	    && !!order.shipped_at
108	    && !order.received_at
109	
110	  const handleCancel = async (reason: string) => {
111	    if (!order) return
112	    setCancelling(true)
113	    try {
114	      const res = await fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${order.id}/cancel`, {
115	        method: 'POST',
116	        credentials: 'same-origin',
117	        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
118	        body: JSON.stringify({ reason }),
119	      })
120	      const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))
121	      if (!res.ok) { alert(json.error || L('Could not cancel the order.', 'ఆర్డర్ రద్దు చేయలేకపోయాం.')); return }
122	      setShowCancel(false)
123	      // Refresh the order so status + any refund show up.
124	      const r = await fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${order.id}`, { credentials: 'same-origin' })
125	      const fresh = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
126	      if (r.ok && fresh.order) setOrder(fresh.order as Order)
127	    } catch {
128	      alert(L('Network error. Please try again.', 'నెట్‌వర్క్ సమస్య. మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.'))
129	    } finally {
130	      setCancelling(false)
131	    }
132	  }
133	
134	  const handleConfirmReceipt = async () => {
135	    if (!order) return
136	    if (!window.confirm(L('Confirm you received this order?', 'మీరు ఈ ఆర్డర్‌ను అందుకున్నారా?'))) return
137	    setConfirmingReceipt(true)
138	    try {
139	      const res = await fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${order.id}/received`, {
140	        method: 'POST',
141	        credentials: 'same-origin',
142	      })
143	      const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))
144	      if (!res.ok) { alert(json.error || L('Could not confirm receipt.', 'అందుకున్నట్టు ధృవీకరించలేకపోయాం.')); return }
145	      const r = await fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${order.id}`, { credentials: 'same-origin' })
146	      const fresh = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
147	      if (r.ok && fresh.order) setOrder(fresh.order as Order)
148	    } catch {
149	      alert(L('Network error. Please try again.', 'నెట్‌వర్క్ సమస్య. మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.'))
150	    } finally {

154	
155	  useEffect(() => {
156	    if (state.status !== 'authenticated' || !id) {
157	      if (state.status === 'anonymous') setLoading(false)
158	      return
159	    }
160	    let cancelled = false
161	    setLoading(true)
162	    setError('')
163	    fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${id}`, { credentials: 'same-origin' })
164	      .then(async (r) => {
165	        const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
166	        if (cancelled) return
167	        if (!r.ok) {
168	          setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not load order.')
169	          setLoading(false)
170	          return
171	        }
172	        setOrder(json.order as Order)
173	        setLoading(false)
174	      })
175	      .catch(() => {
176	        if (cancelled) return
177	        setError('Network error. Please try again.')
178	        setLoading(false)
179	      })
180	    return () => { cancelled = true }
181	  }, [id, state.status])
182	
183	  // The order is "done" once it reaches a terminal state — live polling stops
184	  // there so we're not hitting the API for an order that can't change anymore.
185	  const orderDone = !!order && (
186	    order.status === 'declined'
187	    || order.status === 'cancelled'
188	    || !!order.received_at
189	    || !!order.collected_at
190	    || order.delivery_status === 'delivered'
191	  )
192	  const orderActive = !!order && !orderDone && (order.status === 'pending' || order.status === 'approved')
193	
194	  // Live tracking. While the order screen is open and the order is still active,
195	  // re-fetch every 5s so a farmer/rider action (Shipped, Picked Up, Out for
196	  // delivery…) advances the on-screen timeline almost immediately — and
197	  // re-fetch the moment the buyer brings the tab back into focus. The fetch goes
198	  // through the same secure, ownership-checked API route as the first load; no
199	  // notifications, just the tracker updating itself on screen.
200	  useEffect(() => {
201	    if (!orderActive || !id || state.status !== 'authenticated') return
202	
203	    const silentRefresh = () => {
204	      fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${id}`, { credentials: 'same-origin' })
205	        .then(async (r) => {
206	          if (!r.ok) return
207	          const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
208	          if (json.order) setOrder(json.order as Order)
209	        })
210	        .catch(() => {})
211	    }
212	
213	    const timer = setInterval(silentRefresh, 5000)
214	    const onVisible = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') silentRefresh() }
215	    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
216	    window.addEventListener('focus', silentRefresh)
217	
218	    return () => {
219	      clearInterval(timer)
220	      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
221	      window.removeEventListener('focus', silentRefresh)
222	    }
223	  }, [orderActive, id, state.status])
224	
225	  // Resolve a fresh signed URL for the payment screenshot
226	  useEffect(() => {
227	    if (!order?.payment_proof_path) { setProofUrl(null); return }
228	    let cancelled = false
229	    setProofLoading(true)
230	    fetch(`/api/orders/${order.id}/proof`, { credentials: 'same-origin' })
231	      .then(async (r) => {
232	        const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
233	        if (cancelled) return
234	        setProofUrl(typeof json?.url === 'string' ? json.url : null)
235	        setProofLoading(false)
236	      })
237	      .catch(() => { if (!cancelled) setProofLoading(false) })
238	    return () => { cancelled = true }
239	  }, [order?.id, order?.payment_proof_path])
240	
241	  const statusColor = (s: string) =>
242	    s === 'approved' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
243	      : s === 'declined' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
244	      : s === 'cancelled' ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-700'
245	      : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
246	
247	  const statusLabel = (s: string) =>
248	    s === 'approved' ? tx.statusConfirmed
249	      : s === 'declined' ? tx.statusDeclined
250	      : s === 'cancelled' ? tx.statusCancelled
251	      : tx.statusPending
252	
253	  const paymentLabel = (o: Order) => {
254	    if (o.payment_method === 'cod') return L('Cash on Delivery', 'నగదు చెల్లింపు')
255	    if (isOnlinePayment(o.payment_method)) {
256	      // Show the real method (PhonePe / Google Pay / UPI…) once we know it,
257	      // instead of the gateway name. Falls back to "UPI" before it resolves.
258	      const m = o.payment_method_detail || 'UPI'
259	      if (o.payment_status === 'paid' || o.payment_status === 'completed') return `${m} ✓ Paid`
260	      if (o.payment_status === 'pending_confirmation' || o.payment_status === 'payment_claimed')
261	        return `${m} ⏳ Awaiting farmer confirmation`
262	      if (o.payment_status === 'failed') return `${m} ✕ Not received`
263	      return `${m} · Pending`
264	    }
265	    return o.payment_method ?? '—'
266	  }
267	
268	  const orderDate = order
269	    ? new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
270	        day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
271	      })
272	    : ''
273	  const orderTime = order
274	    ? new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', {
275	        hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
276	      })
277	    : ''
278	
279	  const whatsappHref = order?.farmer?.phone && order?.produce_name
280	    ? `https://wa.me/91${order.farmer.phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
281	        `Hello ${order.farmer.name} anna, regarding my order of ${order.produce_name} on ${orderDate}.`,
282	      )}`
283	    : null
284	
285	  return (
286	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
287	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
288	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
289	          <Link href="/consumer/orders" className="text-green-300 text-sm flex items-center gap-1">
290	            ← {L('Back', 'వెనక్కు')}
291	          </Link>
292	          <LanguageToggle />
293	        </div>
294	        <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">
295	          {L('Order details', 'ఆర్డర్ వివరాలు')}
296	        </h1>
297	      </div>
298	
299	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4 max-w-lg mx-auto">
300	        {state.status === 'anonymous' ? (
301	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-center space-y-4">
302	            <div className="text-5xl">🔒</div>
303	            <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">Log in to view this order</p>
304	            <button
305	              onClick={openAuth}
306	              className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800"
307	            >
308	              {L('Log in', 'లాగిన్')}
309	            </button>
310	          </div>
311	        ) : loading ? (
312	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
313	            {L('Loading...', 'లోడ్ అవుతోంది')}
314	          </div>
315	        ) : error ? (
316	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-red-100 p-6 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</div>
317	        ) : !order ? null : (
318	          <>
319	            {/* Status + payment summary */}
320	            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3">
321	              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
322	                <div>
323	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Order</p>
324	                  {order.order_code && (
325	                    <p className="text-[11px] font-mono font-semibold text-gray-400">{order.order_code}</p>
326	                  )}
327	                  <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-900 leading-tight">
328	                    {localizeName(order.produce_name, lang) || '—'}
329	                  </p>
330	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
331	                    {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}
332	                    {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}
333	                  </p>
334	                </div>
335	                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap ${statusColor(order.status)}`}>
336	                  {statusLabel(order.status)}
337	                </span>
338	              </div>
339	
340	              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1 border-t border-gray-100 mt-3">
341	                <div>
342	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Placed</p>
343	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">{orderDate}</p>
344	                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">{orderTime}</p>
345	                </div>
346	                <div>
347	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Payment</p>
348	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">{paymentLabel(order)}</p>
349	                </div>
350	              </div>
351	
352	              {order.pickup_location && (
353	                <div className="pt-1 border-t border-gray-100 mt-1">
354	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Pickup</p>
355	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">📍 {order.pickup_location}</p>
356	                </div>
357	              )}
358	
359	              {(order.payment_status === 'paid' || order.payment_status === 'completed') && (
360	                <button
361	                  onClick={() => setShowReceipt(true)}
362	                  className="mt-3 w-full border border-green-600 text-green-700 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-50"
363	                >
364	                  🧾 {L('View receipt', 'రసీదు చూడండి')}
365	                </button>
366	              )}
367	
368	              {canCancel && (
369	                <button
370	                  onClick={() => setShowCancel(true)}
371	                  disabled={cancelling}
372	                  className="mt-2 w-full border border-red-300 text-red-600 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-50"
373	                >
374	                  {cancelling ? L('Cancelling...', 'రద్దు చేస్తోంది...') : L('✕ Cancel order', 'ఆర్డర్ రద్దు (30 min లోపు)')}
375	                </button>
376	              )}
377	
378	              {canConfirmReceipt && (
379	                <button
380	                  onClick={handleConfirmReceipt}
381	                  disabled={confirmingReceipt}
382	                  className="mt-3 w-full bg-green-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50"
383	                >
384	                  {confirmingReceipt ? '…' : order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
385	                    ? L('✓ Mark as Delivered', '✓ డెలివరీ అయింది')
386	                    : L('✓ Received', 'అందుకున్నాను')}
387	                </button>
388	              )}
389	            </div>
390	
391	            {/* Pickup / Delivery date the farmer scheduled for this order. */}
392	            {order.fulfillment_date && order.status !== 'declined' && order.status !== 'cancelled' && (
393	              <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
394	                <span className="text-2xl">📅</span>
395	                <div>
396	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-green-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
397	                    {order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
398	                      ? L('Delivery date', 'డెలివరీ తేదీ')
399	                      : L('Pickup date', 'పికప్ తేదీ')}
400	                  </p>
401	                  <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-green-900 mt-0.5">
402	                    {new Date(`${order.fulfillment_date}T00:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
403	                      weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
404	                    })}
405	                  </p>
406	                </div>
407	              </div>
408	            )}
409	
410	            {/* Rider tracking + handover OTP — only for rider-driven home
411	                deliveries (a rider is assigned). Hidden once the order is
412	                declined or cancelled — there's no delivery to track. */}
413	            {riderAssigned && order.status !== 'declined' && order.status !== 'cancelled' && (
414	              <DeliveryPanel order={order} />
415	            )}
416	
417	            {/* Status timeline for everything else: self-pickup, courier, and
418	                farmer-shipped home deliveries (Shipped → Received). Hidden for a
419	                declined/cancelled order — that timeline never happened. */}
420	            {!riderAssigned && order.status !== 'declined' && order.status !== 'cancelled' && (
421	              <OrderStatusPanel order={order} />
422	            )}
423	
424	            {/* Rate the produce — only once the order is completed. */}
425	            {order.produce_listing_id
426	              && order.status !== 'declined'
427	              && order.status !== 'cancelled'
428	              && (!!order.received_at || !!order.collected_at || !!order.delivered_at || order.delivery_status === 'delivered') && (
429	              <ProduceReviewBox
430	                orderId={order.id}
431	                produceName={order.produce_name ? localizeName(order.produce_name, lang) : L('this produce', 'ఈ ఉత్పత్తి')}
432	                existing={order.my_review ?? null}
433	              />
434	            )}
435	
436	            {/* Decline reason */}
437	            {order.status === 'declined' && order.decline_reason && (
438	              <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
439	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 uppercase tracking-wide">{L('Decline reason', 'కారణం')}</p>
440	                <p className="text-sm text-red-800 mt-1 leading-snug">{order.decline_reason}</p>
441	              </div>
442	            )}
443	
444	            {/* Cancelled banner + reason */}
445	            {order.status === 'cancelled' && (
446	              <div className="bg-gray-100 border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
447	                <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-gray-700">{L('✕ Order cancelled', '✕ ఆర్డర్ రద్దు అయింది')}</p>
448	                {order.decline_reason && (
449	                  <>
450	                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide mt-2">{L('Reason', 'కారణం')}</p>
451	                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 mt-0.5 leading-snug">{order.decline_reason}</p>
452	                  </>
453	                )}
454	              </div>
455	            )}
456	
457	            {/* Refund status — shown whenever a refund exists for this order */}
458	            {order.refund_status && <RefundPanel order={order} />}
459	
460	            {/* Payment screenshot */}
461	            {order.payment_method === 'upi' && (
462	              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-2">
463	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
464	                  {L('Payment screenshot', 'చెల్లింపు స్క్రీన్‌షాట్')}
465	                </p>
466	                {!order.payment_proof_path ? (
467	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">No screenshot uploaded yet.</p>
468	                ) : proofLoading ? (
469	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Loading...</p>
470	                ) : proofUrl ? (
471	                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
472	                  <img
473	                    src={proofUrl}
474	                    alt="Payment screenshot"
475	                    className="w-full max-h-96 object-contain rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50"
476	                  />
477	                ) : (
478	                  <p className="text-xs text-red-600">Could not load screenshot. Please refresh.</p>
479	                )}
480	              </div>
481	            )}
482	
483	            {/* Farmer details */}
484	            {order.farmer && (
485	              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3">
486	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
487	                  {L('Farmer', 'రైతు')}
488	                </p>
489	                <div>
490	                  <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-900 leading-tight">🧑‍🌾 {order.farmer.name}</p>
491	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">📍 {order.farmer.village}</p>
492	                </div>
493	                <Link
494	                  href={`/farmer/${order.farmer.slug}`}
495	                  className="block text-center text-sm font-bold text-green-700 underline"
496	                >
497	                  {L('View farm profile', 'రైతు ప్రొఫైల్ చూడండి ↗')}
498	                </Link>
499	                {whatsappHref && (
500	                  <a
501	                    href={whatsappHref}
502	                    target="_blank"
503	                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
504	                    className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
505	                  >
506	                    💬 {L('Contact Farmer', 'రైతును సంప్రదించు')}
507	                  </a>
508	                )}
509	              </div>
510	            )}
511	
512	            {/* Report a problem — pins the complaint to this order automatically */}
513	            <button
514	              onClick={() => setShowComplaint(true)}
515	              className="w-full border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-gray-50"
516	            >
517	              🛟 {L('Report a problem', 'సమస్యను నివేదించండి')}
518	            </button>
519	          </>
520	        )}
521	      </div>
522	
523	      {showReceipt && order && (
524	        <ReceiptOverlay order={order} onClose={() => setShowReceipt(false)} />
525	      )}
526	
527	      {showComplaint && order && (
528	        <ComplaintModal
529	          presetOrderCode={order.order_code}
530	          onClose={() => setShowComplaint(false)}
531	          onCreated={() => setShowComplaint(false)}
532	        />
533	      )}
534	
535	      {showCancel && order && (
536	        <CancelModal
537	          cancelling={cancelling}
538	          onClose={() => setShowCancel(false)}
539	          onConfirm={handleCancel}
540	        />
541	      )}
542	    </main>
543	  )
544	}

546	// Lets the buyer pick / type a reason before cancelling. The reason is stored on
547	// the order and shown to the farmer, so they understand why it fell through.
548	function CancelModal({
549	  cancelling,
550	  onClose,
551	  onConfirm,
552	}: {
553	  cancelling: boolean
554	  onClose: () => void
555	  onConfirm: (reason: string) => void
556	}) {
557	  const { L } = useLang()
558	  const presets = [
559	    L('Ordered by mistake', 'పొరపాటున ఆర్డర్ చేశాను'),
560	    L('Found it cheaper elsewhere', 'వేరే చోట చౌకగా దొరికింది'),
561	    L('No longer needed', 'ఇప్పుడు అవసరం లేదు'),
562	    L('Delivery / pickup takes too long', 'డెలివరీ / పికప్ చాలా ఆలస్యం'),
563	  ]
564	  const [reason, setReason] = useState('')
565	
566	  return (
567	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-4">
568	      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-5 space-y-4">
569	        <div>
570	          <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-900">{L('Cancel order', 'ఆర్డర్ రద్దు')}</p>
571	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1 leading-snug">
572	            {L('Please tell the farmer why. If you have already paid, you will be refunded automatically.', 'రైతుకు కారణం చెప్పండి. మీరు ఇప్పటికే చెల్లించి ఉంటే, డబ్బు ఆటోమేటిక్‌గా తిరిగి వస్తుంది.')}
573	          </p>
574	        </div>
575	
576	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
577	          {presets.map((p) => (
578	            <button
579	              key={p}
580	              type="button"
581	              onClick={() => setReason(p)}
582	              className={`text-[11px] font-semibold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border ${
583	                reason === p
584	                  ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-green-700'
585	                  : 'bg-gray-50 text-gray-700 border-gray-200 active:bg-gray-100'
586	              }`}
587	            >
588	              {p}
589	            </button>
590	          ))}
591	        </div>
592	
593	        <textarea
594	          value={reason}
595	          onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
596	          rows={3}
597	          maxLength={300}
598	          placeholder={L('Reason (optional)', 'కారణం (ఐచ్ఛికం)')}
599	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none resize-none"
600	        />
601	
602	        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
603	          <button
604	            onClick={onClose}
605	            disabled={cancelling}
606	            className="border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-50"
607	          >
608	            {L('Keep order', 'ఆర్డర్ ఉంచు')}
609	          </button>
610	          <button
611	            onClick={() => onConfirm(reason.trim())}
612	            disabled={cancelling}
613	            className="bg-red-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-50"
614	          >
615	            {cancelling ? L('Cancelling...', 'రద్దు...') : L('Cancel order', 'రద్దు చేయి')}
616	          </button>
617	        </div>
618	      </div>
619	    </div>
620	  )
621	}
622	
623	// Printable payment receipt. Shown as an overlay; the print stylesheet hides
624	// everything except .yff-receipt so "Print / Save as PDF" produces a clean
625	// one-page receipt on both phones and laptops.
626	function ReceiptOverlay({ order, onClose }: { order: Order; onClose: () => void }) {
627	  const { lang, L } = useLang()
628	  const placed = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleString('en-IN', {
629	    day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
630	  })
631	  const paymentRef = order.razorpay_payment_id || order.order_code || order.id
632	
633	  return (
634	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
635	      <style>{`@media print {

685	function DeliveryPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
686	  const { L } = useLang()
687	  const ds: DeliveryStatus = (order.delivery_status as DeliveryStatus) || 'unassigned'
688	
689	  const steps: Array<{ key: DeliveryStatus; label: string; sub: string; at: string | null | undefined }> = [
690	    { key: 'unassigned', label: 'Order placed', sub: 'Waiting for a delivery boy', at: order.created_at },
691	    { key: 'assigned', label: 'Rider assigned', sub: 'On the way to farmer', at: order.assigned_at },
692	    { key: 'picked_up', label: 'Picked up', sub: 'Collected from farmer', at: order.picked_up_at },
693	    { key: 'out_for_delivery', label: 'Out for delivery', sub: 'On the way to you', at: order.out_for_delivery_at },
694	    { key: 'delivered', label: 'Delivered', sub: 'Order completed', at: order.delivered_at },
695	  ]
696	
697	  const stageIndex = (k: DeliveryStatus) => ['unassigned', 'assigned', 'picked_up', 'out_for_delivery', 'delivered'].indexOf(k)
698	  const current = stageIndex(ds)
699	
700	  const formatAt = (iso: string | null | undefined) => {
701	    if (!iso) return ''
702	    const d = new Date(iso)
703	    return d.toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
704	  }
705	
706	  return (
707	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-4">
708	      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
709	        <span className="text-base">🛵</span>
710	        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
711	          {L('Home delivery', 'ఇంటికి డెలివరీ')}
712	        </p>
713	      </div>
714	
715	      {/* Handover OTP — visible once a rider has been assigned. Customer
716	          reads this 4-digit code to the rider at the door so the system
717	          can confirm the right person received the goods. */}
718	      {ds !== 'unassigned' && ds !== 'delivered' && order.handover_otp && (
719	        <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
720	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 uppercase tracking-wide">
721	            {L('Read this code to the delivery boy at your door', 'డోర్ వద్ద డెలివరీ బాయ్‌కు ఈ కోడ్ చెప్పండి')}
722	          </p>
723	          <p className="text-4xl font-black tracking-widest text-amber-900 mt-2 font-mono">
724	            {order.handover_otp}
725	          </p>
726	        </div>
727	      )}
728	
729	      {ds === 'delivered' && (
730	        <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
731	          <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800">✓ Delivered</p>
732	          <p className="text-xs text-green-700 mt-0.5">{formatAt(order.delivered_at)}</p>
733	        </div>
734	      )}
735	
736	      {/* Status timeline */}
737	      <ol className="space-y-2">
738	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
739	          const reached = idx <= current
740	          const isCurrent = idx === current
741	          return (
742	            <li key={s.key} className="flex items-start gap-3">
743	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
744	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-green-200' : ''}`} />
745	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
746	                  <span className={`w-0.5 flex-1 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
747	                )}
748	              </div>
749	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
750	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{s.label}</p>
751	                <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
752	                {s.at && reached && (
753	                  <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{formatAt(s.at)}</p>
754	                )}
755	              </div>
756	            </li>
757	          )
758	        })}
759	      </ol>
760	
761	      {/* Delivery address (always visible — confirms what consumer entered) */}
762	      {order.delivery_address && (
763	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
764	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-1">Delivering to</p>
765	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-800 leading-snug whitespace-pre-line">{order.delivery_address}</p>
766	          {order.delivery_landmark && (
767	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-0.5">📍 {order.delivery_landmark}</p>
768	          )}
769	          {order.delivery_pincode && (
770	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">PIN: {order.delivery_pincode}</p>
771	          )}
772	        </div>
773	      )}
774	
775	      {/* Rider contact — only when assigned (and not yet delivered) */}
776	      {order.rider && ds !== 'delivered' && (
777	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
778	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide">{L('Delivery boy', 'డెలివరీ బాయ్')}</p>
779	          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mt-1">{order.rider.name || 'Your delivery partner'}</p>
780	          <a
781	            href={`tel:${order.rider.phone}`}
782	            className="mt-2 w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-blue-700"
783	          >
784	            📞 Call · {order.rider.phone}
785	          </a>
786	        </div>
787	      )}
788	    </div>
789	  )
790	}

792	// Order status timeline for self-pickup and courier orders. Home delivery has
793	// its own richer DeliveryPanel; this covers the pickup and courier lifecycles.
794	// Courier diverges after "Confirmed": Shipped → Received instead of Ready →
795	// Picked up, so a courier buyer sees the right milestones (and dates).
796	function OrderStatusPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
797	  const { L } = useLang()
798	  const approved = order.status === 'approved'
799	  // Shipped lifecycle (Shipped → Received) covers courier AND farmer-shipped
800	  // home deliveries. Rider home deliveries never reach this panel. Self-pickup
801	  // keeps the Ready → Picked up lifecycle.
802	  const isShippedFlow = order.delivery_type === 'courier' || order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
803	  const isCourier = isShippedFlow
804	  const collected = !!order.collected_at
805	  const shipped = !!order.shipped_at
806	  const received = !!order.received_at
807	  // Online orders that have been paid show an extra "Payment received" step.
808	  const paidOnline = isOnlinePayment(order.payment_method)
809	    && (order.payment_status === 'paid' || order.payment_status === 'completed')
810	
811	  const fmt = (iso?: string | null) =>
812	    iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : ''
813	
814	  type Step = { label: string; sub: string; at: string; done: boolean }
815	  const steps: Step[] = [
816	    { label: L('Order placed', 'ఆర్డర్ పెట్టారు'), sub: L('We received your order', 'మీ ఆర్డర్ అందింది'), at: fmt(order.created_at), done: true },
817	    ...(paidOnline
818	      ? [{ label: L('Payment received', 'చెల్లింపు అందింది'), sub: `${order.payment_method_detail || 'UPI'} payment confirmed / చెల్లింపు ధృవీకరించబడింది`, at: fmt(order.paid_at), done: true }]
819	      : []),
820	    { label: L('Confirmed by farmer', 'రైతు ధృవీకరించారు'), sub: L('Farmer accepted your order', 'రైతు అంగీకరించారు'), at: fmt(order.confirmed_at), done: approved },
821	    ...(isCourier
822	      ? [
823	          { label: L('Shipped', 'షిప్ చేయబడింది'), sub: L('Farmer shipped your order to your address', 'రైతు మీ చిరునామాకు పంపారు'), at: fmt(order.shipped_at), done: shipped },
824	          { label: L('Received', 'అందుకున్నారు'), sub: L('You confirmed you received it', 'మీరు అందుకున్నట్టు ధృవీకరించారు'), at: fmt(order.received_at), done: received },
825	        ]
826	      : [
827	          { label: L('Ready for pickup', 'తీసుకెళ్లడానికి సిద్ధం'), sub: order.pickup_location ? `Collect at ${order.pickup_location}` : L('Collect from the farmer', 'రైతు నుండి తీసుకోండి'), at: '', done: approved },
828	          { label: L('Picked up', 'తీసుకున్నారు'), sub: L('Collection confirmed', 'తీసుకున్నట్టు ధృవీకరించారు'), at: fmt(order.collected_at), done: collected },
829	        ]),
830	  ]
831	
832	  // Current = the furthest milestone reached (last step marked done).
833	  let current = 0
834	  steps.forEach((s, i) => { if (s.done) current = i })
835	
836	  return (
837	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-4">
838	      {/* Header — mirrors the home-delivery panel so every order type's tracker
839	          looks the same, just with the icon/label for this fulfilment type. */}
840	      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
841	        <span className="text-base">{order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery' ? '🛵' : isShippedFlow ? '📦' : '🧺'}</span>
842	        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
843	          {order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
844	            ? L('Home delivery', 'ఇంటి డెలివరీ')
845	            : isShippedFlow ? L('Courier delivery', 'కొరియర్ డెలివరీ') : L('Farm pickup', 'ఫామ్ పికప్')}
846	        </p>
847	      </div>
848	
849	      {/* Prominent completion banner once the order is resolved — same style as
850	          the "Delivered" banner on the home-delivery tracker. */}
851	      {(received || collected) && (
852	        <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
853	          <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800">
854	            {isCourier ? `✓ ${L('Received', 'అందుకున్నారు')}` : `✓ ${L('Picked up', 'తీసుకున్నారు')}`}
855	          </p>
856	          <p className="text-xs text-green-700 mt-0.5">{fmt(isCourier ? order.received_at : order.collected_at)}</p>
857	        </div>
858	      )}
859	
860	      {/* Handover code — shown once the order is ready and before it's
861	          collected. The customer reads it to the farmer at pickup. */}
862	      {approved && !collected && order.handover_otp && (
863	        <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
864	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 uppercase tracking-wide">
865	            {L('Show this code to the farmer when you collect', 'తీసుకునేటప్పుడు ఈ కోడ్ రైతుకు చెప్పండి')}
866	          </p>
867	          <p className="text-4xl font-black tracking-widest text-amber-900 mt-2 font-mono">
868	            {order.handover_otp}
869	          </p>
870	        </div>
871	      )}
872	
873	      <ol className="space-y-2">
874	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
875	          const reached = idx <= current
876	          const isCurrent = idx === current
877	          return (
878	            <li key={s.label} className="flex items-start gap-3">
879	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
880	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-green-200' : ''}`} />
881	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
882	                  <span className={`w-0.5 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
883	                )}
884	              </div>
885	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
886	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{s.label}</p>
887	                <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
888	                {s.at && reached && <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{s.at}</p>}
889	              </div>
890	            </li>
891	          )
892	        })}
893	      </ol>
894	    </div>
895	  )
896	}

898	// Refund timeline. Maps the stored refund_status (our manual 'initiated', or
899	// a Razorpay status: 'pending' / 'processed' / 'failed') onto a simple
900	// buyer-facing progression: Initiated → Processing → Credited.
901	function RefundPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
902	  const { L } = useLang()
903	  const rs = (order.refund_status || '').toLowerCase()
904	  const failed = rs === 'failed'
905	  const amount = order.refund_amount ?? order.total_price ?? 0
906	
907	  // current = how far along the 3-step bar we are.
908	  // initiated → 0, pending → 1, processed → 2 (credited).
909	  const current = rs === 'processed' ? 2 : rs === 'pending' ? 1 : 0
910	
911	  const steps = [
912	    { label: L('Refund initiated', 'రీఫండ్ మొదలైంది'), sub: L('We started your refund', 'మీ రీఫండ్ ప్రారంభించాం') },
913	    { label: L('Processing', 'ప్రాసెస్ అవుతోంది'), sub: L('Sent to your bank/UPI', 'మీ బ్యాంక్/UPIకి పంపాం') },
914	    { label: L('Credited', 'జమ అయింది'), sub: L('Reflects in 3–5 business days', '3–5 పని రోజుల్లో కనిపిస్తుంది') },
915	  ]
916	
917	  const refundedAt = order.refunded_at
918	    ? new Date(order.refunded_at).toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
919	    : null
920	
921	  if (failed) {
922	    return (
923	      <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-1.5">
924	        <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-800">
925	          {L('⚠️ Refund failed', 'రీఫండ్ విఫలమైంది')}
926	        </span>
927	        <p className="text-sm text-red-800 leading-snug">
928	          {L(`Your refund of ₹${amount} could not be processed. Please contact support and we'll fix it.`, `మీ ₹${amount} రీఫండ్ ప్రాసెస్ కాలేదు. దయచేసి సపోర్ట్‌ను సంప్రదించండి, మేము సరి చేస్తాం.`)}
929	        </p>
930	      </div>
931	    )
932	  }
933	
934	  return (
935	    <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3">
936	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
937	        <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-purple-100 text-purple-800">
938	          💸 Refund of ₹{amount}
939	        </span>
940	        {refundedAt && <span className="text-[10px] text-purple-500">{refundedAt}</span>}
941	      </div>
942	
943	      <ol className="space-y-2">
944	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
945	          const reached = idx <= current
946	          const isCurrent = idx === current
947	          return (
948	            <li key={s.label} className="flex items-start gap-3">
949	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
950	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-purple-600' : 'bg-purple-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-purple-200' : ''}`} />
951	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
952	                  <span className={`w-0.5 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-purple-600' : 'bg-purple-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
953	                )}
954	              </div>
955	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
956	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-purple-900' : 'text-purple-300'}`}>{s.label}</p>
957	                <p className="text-[10px] text-purple-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
958	              </div>
959	            </li>
960	          )
961	        })}
962	      </ol>
963	
964	      {order.refund_id && (
965	        <p className="text-[10px] text-purple-400 font-mono border-t border-purple-100 pt-2">Ref: {order.refund_id}</p>
966	      )}
967	    </div>
968	  )

8	import ComplaintModal from '@/components/consumer/ComplaintModal'
9	import OrderFeedbackModal from '@/components/consumer/OrderFeedbackModal'
10	import OrderCard, { ConsumerOrder as Order, isResolved, isCompleted } from '@/components/consumer/OrderCard'
11	import CancelOrderModal, { CancelSuccessSheet } from '@/components/consumer/CancelOrderModal'
12	
13	export default function ConsumerOrdersPage() {
14	  const { tx, L } = useLang()
15	  const { state, openAuth } = useConsumerAuth()
16	  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Order[]>([])
17	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
18	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
19	  // Order code the complaint modal is pinned to ('' means a general complaint
20	  // with no preset). null means the modal is closed.
21	  const [complaintFor, setComplaintFor] = useState<string | null>(null)
22	  // Order the feedback sheet is open for (null = closed).
23	  const [feedbackFor, setFeedbackFor] = useState<Order | null>(null)
24	  // Order id currently mid-request (acknowledge / confirm receipt).
25	  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null)
26	  // Order the cancel-reason modal is open for (null = closed) + its busy flag.
27	  const [cancellingOrder, setCancellingOrder] = useState<Order | null>(null)
28	  const [cancelBusy, setCancelBusy] = useState(false)
29	  // After a successful cancel, show the animated confirmation ({ wasPaid }).
30	  const [cancelledInfo, setCancelledInfo] = useState<{ wasPaid: boolean } | null>(null)
31	
32	  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
33	    setLoading(true)
34	    setError('')
35	    const r = await fetch('/api/consumer/orders', { credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)
36	    if (!r) { setError('Could not load orders. Check your connection.'); setLoading(false); return }
37	    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
38	    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not load orders.'); setLoading(false); return }
39	    setOrders((json.orders ?? []) as Order[])
40	    setLoading(false)
41	  }, [])
42	
43	  useEffect(() => {
44	    if (state.status === 'authenticated') {
45	      void refresh()
46	    } else if (state.status === 'anonymous') {
47	      setLoading(false)
48	    }
49	  }, [state.status, refresh])
50	
51	  // Buyer acknowledges a farmer-declined order — moves it to history.
52	  const acknowledge = useCallback(async (order: Order) => {
53	    setBusyId(order.id)
54	    const r = await fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${order.id}/acknowledge`, {
55	      method: 'POST',
56	      credentials: 'same-origin',
57	    }).catch(() => null)
58	    if (r && r.ok) await refresh()
59	    else alert(L('Could not update. Please try again.', 'నవీకరించలేకపోయాం. మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.'))
60	    setBusyId(null)
61	  }, [refresh, L])
62	
63	  // Buyer confirms a shipped order was delivered/received — moves it to history.
64	  const confirmReceipt = useCallback(async (order: Order) => {
65	    if (!window.confirm(L('Confirm you received this order?', 'మీరు ఈ ఆర్డర్‌ను అందుకున్నారా?'))) return
66	    setBusyId(order.id)
67	    const r = await fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${order.id}/received`, {
68	      method: 'POST',
69	      credentials: 'same-origin',
70	    }).catch(() => null)
71	    if (r && r.ok) await refresh()
72	    else alert(L('Could not confirm. Please try again.', 'ధృవీకరించలేకపోయాం. మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.'))
73	    setBusyId(null)
74	  }, [refresh, L])
75	
76	  // Buyer cancels a still-pending order with an optional reason. On success the
77	  // order becomes 'cancelled' (and any paid amount is refunded by the backend),
78	  // then it surfaces on the farmer's dashboard for them to acknowledge.
79	  const cancelOrder = useCallback(async (reason: string) => {
80	    if (!cancellingOrder) return
81	    setCancelBusy(true)
82	    const r = await fetch(`/api/consumer/orders/${cancellingOrder.id}/cancel`, {
83	      method: 'POST',
84	      credentials: 'same-origin',
85	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
86	      body: JSON.stringify({ reason }),
87	    }).catch(() => null)
88	    const json = r ? await r.json().catch(() => ({})) : null
89	    if (r && r.ok) {
90	      // A paid order is refunded automatically — drives the refund note.
91	      const wasPaid = ['paid', 'completed', 'payment_claimed', 'pending_confirmation'].includes(
92	        cancellingOrder.payment_status ?? '',
93	      )
94	      setCancellingOrder(null)
95	      setCancelledInfo({ wasPaid })
96	      await refresh()
97	    } else {
98	      alert(json?.error ?? L('Could not cancel. Please try again.', 'రద్దు చేయలేకపోయాం. మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.'))
99	    }
100	    setCancelBusy(false)
101	  }, [cancellingOrder, refresh, L])
102	
103	  // Active orders only — resolved ones (delivered / picked up / received, or an
104	  // acknowledged decline / cancel) live on the separate history page. A declined
105	  // or cancelled order that's NOT yet acknowledged stays here so the buyer sees it.
106	  const activeOrders = orders.filter((o) => !isResolved(o))
107	  const completedCount = orders.length - activeOrders.length
108	
109	  return (
110	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
111	      {/* Header */}
112	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
113	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
114	          <Link href="/consumer" className="text-green-300 text-sm flex items-center gap-1">
115	            {L('← Back', '← వెనక్కు')}
116	          </Link>
117	          <LanguageToggle />
118	        </div>
119	        <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">{tx.myOrders}</h1>
120	        <p className="text-green-400 text-sm mt-1">
121	          {L('Your orders in progress', 'మీ ప్రస్తుత ఆర్డర్‌లు')}
122	        </p>
123	      </div>
124	
125	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4 max-w-lg mx-auto">
126	        {/* Active | History switcher — two separate pages, shown side by side
127	            so finished orders are one tap away without cluttering this list. */}
128	        {state.status === 'authenticated' && !error && (
129	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-1.5 flex gap-1.5">
130	            <span className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-center bg-green-700 text-white">
131	              {L('Active', 'ప్రస్తుత')} ({activeOrders.length})
132	            </span>
133	            <Link
134	              href="/consumer/orders/history"
135	              className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-center bg-gray-50 text-gray-600 active:bg-gray-100"
136	            >
137	              {L('History', 'చరిత్ర')}{completedCount > 0 ? ` (${completedCount})` : ''}
138	            </Link>
139	          </div>
140	        )}
141	
142	        {state.status === 'loading' || loading ? (
143	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
144	            {L('Loading...', 'లోడ్ అవుతోంది')}
145	          </div>
146	        ) : state.status === 'anonymous' ? (
147	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-center space-y-4">
148	            <div className="text-5xl">🔒</div>
149	            <div>
150	              <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">
151	                {L('Log in to see your orders', 'మీ ఆర్డర్‌లు చూడటానికి లాగిన్ అవ్వండి')}
152	              </p>
153	            </div>
154	            <button
155	              onClick={openAuth}
156	              className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800"
157	            >
158	              {L('Log in', 'లాగిన్')}
159	            </button>
160	          </div>
161	        ) : error ? (
162	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-red-100 p-6 space-y-3">
163	            <p className="text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
164	            <button
165	              onClick={() => void refresh()}
166	              className="text-sm font-bold text-green-700 underline"
167	            >
168	              {L('Try again', 'మళ్లీ ప్రయత్నించండి')}
169	            </button>
170	          </div>
171	        ) : orders.length === 0 ? (
172	          <div className="text-center py-14">
173	            <div className="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
174	            <p className="font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm">{tx.noOrdersYet}</p>
175	            <Link href="/consumer" className="mt-4 inline-block text-green-700 text-sm underline font-semibold">
176	              {L('Browse produce →', 'పంట చూడండి →')}
177	            </Link>
178	          </div>
179	        ) : (
180	          <div className="space-y-3">
181	            {activeOrders.length === 0 ? (
182	              <div className="text-center py-12">
183	                <div className="text-4xl mb-2">🧺</div>
184	                <p className="font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm">
185	                  {L('No active orders', 'ప్రస్తుత ఆర్డర్‌లు లేవు')}
186	                </p>
187	                <Link href="/consumer" className="mt-3 inline-block text-green-700 text-sm underline font-semibold">
188	                  {L('Browse produce →', 'పంట చూడండి →')}
189	                </Link>
190	              </div>
191	            ) : (
192	              activeOrders.map((order) => (
193	                <OrderCard
194	                  key={order.id}
195	                  order={order}
196	                  onComplaint={setComplaintFor}
197	                  onFeedback={setFeedbackFor}
198	                  onAcknowledge={acknowledge}
199	                  onConfirmReceipt={confirmReceipt}
200	                  onCancel={setCancellingOrder}
201	                  busy={busyId === order.id}
202	                />
203	              ))
204	            )}
205	          </div>
206	        )}
207	
208	        <Link
209	          href="/consumer/complaints"
210	          className="mt-6 block text-center text-xs font-semibold text-green-700 underline"
211	        >
212	          🛟 {L('My complaints', 'నా ఫిర్యాదులు')}
213	        </Link>
214	
215	        <Link
216	          href="/buyer-protection"
217	          className="mt-3 block text-center text-xs font-semibold text-green-700 underline"
218	        >
219	          🔒 {L('Buyer protection & refund policy', 'కొనుగోలుదారు రక్షణ & రీఫండ్ విధానం')}
220	        </Link>
221	      </div>
222	
223	      {complaintFor !== null && (
224	        <ComplaintModal

228	        />

230	
231	      {feedbackFor && (

233	          orderId={feedbackFor.id}
234	          produceName={feedbackFor.produce_name}

239	        />

253	          onClose={() => setCancelledInfo(null)}

label: L('Picked up by buyer', 'కొనుగోలుదారు తీసుకున్నారు'), at: order.collected_at, done: !!order.collected_at },\n        ]\n      })()\n    : []\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"min-h-screen text-gray-400 uppercase\">{L('Placed', 'పెట్టారు')}</p>\n                  <p className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700\">{fmt(order.created_at)}</p>\n                </div>\n }

> AGENT

The build succeeds and the new `/farmer/dashboard/orders/[id]` route is registered. Everything compiles and lints clean.

## What I did

**1. New farmer order detail page** — `src/app/farmer/dashboard/orders/[id]/page.tsx`

A read-only page that shows everything about one order:
- **Summary** — produce, quantity, price, status badge, placed date/time, payment method (+ UTR for UPI)
- **Buyer** — name, tap-to-call button, alternate phone
- **Fulfilment** — self-pickup location *or* home-delivery/courier address (landmark, PIN), the scheduled pickup/delivery date, and the assigned rider's contact for home deliveries
- **Progress timeline** — order placed → approved → shipped/ready → received/picked-up (or the full rider flow for home deliveries)
- **Decline / cancellation reason** and **refund** info when applicable

It reads the order via the anon Supabase client scoped to `.eq('farmer_id', <localStorage yff_farmer_id>)` — matching the existing farmer-dashboard auth pattern, so a farmer can only ever open their own orders. It deliberately does **not** fetch `handover_otp`, keeping the same security rule the orders list follows (the pickup code must come from the customer).

**2. Made order cards tap-navigable** — across both `OrderCard` (Pending/Approved + unacknowledged-cancelled) and `HistoryCard` (All/Picked up/Declined/Cancelled)

- The order's info region (buyer, produce, price, location) now opens the detail page on tap, with a small green **"View full details →"** cue so it's discoverable.
- The phone-call link inside calls `stopPropagation()`, so tapping it dials instead of navigating.
- All existing action controls (approve, decline, date picker, mark paid, acknowledge, etc.) sit **outside** the tappable region, so they keep working exactly as before.

The order ID fix from the previous turn is intact and verified by the same build.

One note: I kept the detail page **read-only** (a comprehensive view), since the action buttons already live on the list cards. If you'd prefer the approve/decline/mark-picked-up actions to also be available on the detail page itself, say the word and I'll add them.

  "new_string": "    // Online payments come through the Razorpay gateway (stored as 'razorpay';\n    // some legacy orders use 'upi'). Never surface the gateway name \"razorpay\"\n    // to the farmer — show \"UPI\".\n    if (o.payment_method === 'upi' || o.payment_method === 'razorpay')\n      return isPaid ? L('UPI — Paid', 'UPI — చెల్లించారు')\n        : (o.payment_status === 'payment_claimed' || o.payment_status === 'pending_confirmation')\n          ? L('UPI — Buyer paid, verify', 'UPI — ధృవీకరించండి')\n          : L('UPI — Pending', 'UPI — పెండింగ్')\n    return o.payment_method\n  }"
}

1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
3	
4	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
5	import { Suspense } from 'react'
6	import TopNav from '@/components/farmer/TopNav'
7	import FarmCover from '@/components/farmer/FarmCover'
8	import TrustStrip from '@/components/farmer/TrustStrip'
9	import TabSection from '@/components/farmer/TabSection'
10	import { CartFab } from '@/components/consumer/Cart'
11	
12	export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
13	  const { slug } = await params
14	
15	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
16	    .from('farmers')
17	    .select('id, name, village, district, story_quote')
18	    .eq('slug', slug)
19	    .single()
20	
21	  if (!farmer) return { title: 'Farmer not found' }
22	
23	  const { data: firstPhoto } = await supabase
24	    .from('media')
25	    .select('url')
26	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
27	    .eq('type', 'photo')
28	    .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
29	    .limit(1)
30	    .single()
31	
32	  const title = `${farmer.name} — Natural farmer in ${farmer.village}`
33	  const description =
34	    farmer.story_quote ??
35	    `Buy natural produce directly from ${farmer.name}, ${farmer.village}, ${farmer.district}. No middlemen. No chemicals.`
36	
37	  return {
38	    title: `${farmer.name}'s Farm — YourFamilyFarmer`,
39	    description,
40	    openGraph: {
41	      title,
42	      description,
43	      siteName: 'YourFamilyFarmer',
44	      ...(firstPhoto?.url ? { images: [{ url: firstPhoto.url, width: 1200, height: 630, alt: `${farmer.name}'s farm` }] } : {}),
45	    },
46	    twitter: {
47	      card: 'summary_large_image',
48	      title,
49	      description,
50	      ...(firstPhoto?.url ? { images: [firstPhoto.url] } : {}),
51	    },
52	  }
53	}
54	
55	export default async function FarmerPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
56	  const { slug } = await params
57	
58	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
59	    .from('farmers')
60	    .select('*')
61	    .eq('slug', slug)
62	    .eq('active', true)
63	    .single()
64	
65	  if (!farmer) notFound()
66	
67	  const { data: produce } = await supabase
68	    .from('produce_listings')
69	    .select('*')
70	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
71	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
72	
73	  const { data: reviews } = await supabase
74	    .from('reviews')
75	    .select('*')
76	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
77	    .eq('approved', true)
78	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
79	
80	  // Verified per-order buyer feedback (the ⭐ button consumers tap on their
81	  // orders) lives in produce_reviews, tied to a listing. Only feedback on a
82	  // completed order is approved/public. Surface it on the farmer's profile too —
83	  // tagged with the produce it was about — so consumer feedback isn't invisible
84	  // here, and fold it into the same Reviews tab + star average as written reviews.
85	  const { data: produceReviews } = await supabase
86	    .from('produce_reviews')
87	    .select('id, reviewer_name, star_rating, review_text, created_at, produce_listings(name)')
88	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
89	    .eq('approved', true)
90	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
91	
92	  const mappedProduceReviews = (produceReviews ?? []).map((r) => {
93	    const pl = Array.isArray(r.produce_listings) ? r.produce_listings[0] : r.produce_listings
94	    return {
95	      id: r.id,
96	      reviewer_name: r.reviewer_name,
97	      star_rating: r.star_rating,
98	      review_text: r.review_text,
99	      produce_ordered: (pl as { name?: string } | null)?.name ?? null,
100	      created_at: r.created_at,
101	    }
102	  })
103	
104	  // Both sources, newest first — one combined list for the Reviews tab.
105	  const allReviews = [...(reviews ?? []), ...mappedProduceReviews].sort(
106	    (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at as string).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at as string).getTime(),
107	  )
108	
109	  // Star rating shown in the trust strip reflects all real feedback (written
110	  // reviews + verified order feedback). Falls back to the stored value if there
111	  // are no reviews yet.
112	  const ratingAvg =
113	    allReviews.length > 0
114	      ? Math.round(
115	          (allReviews.reduce((sum, r) => sum + ((r.star_rating as number) ?? 0), 0) / allReviews.length) * 10,
116	        ) / 10
117	      : (farmer.rating_avg ?? 0)
118	
119	  const { data: media } = await supabase
120	    .from('media')
121	    .select('*')
122	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
123	    .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
124	
125	  return (
126	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24">
127	      <TopNav regionSlug={farmer.region_slug} />
128	      <FarmCover farmer={farmer} />
129	      {/* Count only live listings — a produce the farmer has suspended (or that
130	          is still "coming soon") must not inflate the public "Produce now" stat. */}
131	      <TrustStrip
132	        farmer={{ ...farmer, rating_avg: ratingAvg }}
133	        produceCount={(produce ?? []).filter((p) => (p as { status?: string }).status === 'available').length}
134	      />
135	      <Suspense fallback={null}>
136	        <TabSection
137	          farmer={farmer}
138	          produce={produce ?? []}
139	          reviews={allReviews}
140	          media={media ?? []}
141	        />
142	      </Suspense>
143	      <CartFab />
144	    </main>
145	  )
146	}
147	
```
